2026-11-22 05:13:24 JST [akira/akira/a5d9bcf7769c431e9ecf056f6f857f99] Actually wait — 183 involves deciding vocabulary. I must decide: 「現行」→ keep as is meaning "現在提供中（最新世代かどうかは問わない）"? Or change to 「提供中」? Let me think about what's best for readers: In pricing tables, state column values like 現行／旧版 are ambiguous. Better: 「提供中」「旧版（後継あり）」「提供終了」「限定提供」「プレビュー」. The term 「現行」implies latest. Since 11-21 they added a 状態列 to /kimi-k3-1-pro/ with kimi-k3=旧版. I'll define:
2026-11-22 05:13:24 JST [akira/akira/a5d9bcf7769c431e9ecf056f6f857f99] - 提供中 = 現在APIで利用可能（世代の新旧を問わない）
2026-11-22 05:13:24 JST [akira/akira/a5d9bcf7769c431e9ecf056f6f857f99] - 旧版 = 後継モデルが公開済みで、自らは旧世代
2026-11-22 05:13:24 JST [akira/akira/a5d9bcf7769c431e9ecf056f6f857f99] - 終了 = 提供終了
2026-11-22 05:13:24 JST [akira/akira/a5d9bcf7769c431e9ecf056f6f857f99] - 限定提供 = 承認制・招待制など一般提供でない
2026-11-22 05:13:24 JST [akira/akira/a5d9bcf7769c431e9ecf056f6f857f99] - プレビュー = プレビュー段階
2026-11-22 05:13:24 JST [akira/akira/a5d9bcf7769c431e9ecf056f6f857f99] Hmm but "提供中" vs "旧版" is contradictory: 旧版 is also 提供中. Better: the state column expresses availability *and* generation. Two things in one column → confusion. Decision: 状態列の語彙は 4値: 「提供中(最新)」「提供中(旧版)」... too clunky.
2026-11-22 05:13:24 JST [akira/akira/a5d9bcf7769c431e9ecf056f6f857f99] Cleaner: 「現行」= その提供者の最新世代として提供中、「旧版」= 提供中だが後継あり、「終了」= 提供終了、「限定提供」= 一般提供前/承認制. That's what's already effectively in use. So decision 183 = 「現行」は"最新世代かつ提供中"の意味に限定し、後継があるものは必ず「旧版」にする。語彙は 現行／旧版／終了／限定提供／プレビュー の5値で固定。台帳 schema_note と SITE_RULES に書く。

2026-11-22 05:14:37 JST [akira/akira/a5d9bcf7769c431e9ecf056f6f857f99] Current state: the only non-5-value is "提供終了予定" (Gemini 3.7 Flash). Under the new 5-value vocabulary, "提供終了予定" (sunset announced but still serving) — the definitions given: 現行 = latest generation of that provider AND currently served via API; 旧版 = still served but successor exists; 終了 = serving ended; 限定提供 = trusted-access; プレビュー = preview.
2026-11-22 05:14:37 JST [akira/akira/a5d9bcf7769c431e9ecf056f6f857f99] Gemini 3.7 Flash: there's a successor (3.8 Flash), still serving until 2027-01-28 → 旧版. The "提供終了予定" nuance can go into the note column, not the status column.

2026-11-22 05:22:36 JST [akira/akira/a5d9bcf7769c431e9ecf056f6f857f99] 346:\* Billing is based on emitted video output tokens at `240`tokens per frame (`5,760` tokens per second at `24 fps`), with `48 kHz` stereoaudio bundled into the video token rate. Under Standard pricing (`$17.50` per 1Mvideo output tokens), this equates to an effective rate of approximately `$0.10`per second (`~$6.05` per minute). Always call `complete()` to end an activesession and stop billing. Genie 4 does not support the Free tier, Batch API,Flex inference, or Priority inference.

2026-11-22 05:22:36 JST [akira/akira/a5d9bcf7769c431e9ecf056f6f857f99] - **Agentic video understanding:** When using agentic video understanding,token usage is variable based on the content loaded by the model rather thanfull video length. This typically results in up to 88% fewer input tokensfor long-form video, though token counts depend on query complexity anddynamic sampling depth (which may exceed 1 FPS for detailed visualsegments). See [Agentic videounderstanding](https://aistudio.google.com/docs/video-understanding#agentic-video-understanding).
